- **binary-classifier-metric-computation**: Use when a per-read binary modification classifier (e.g. CHEUI-solo Model 1 CNN outputs on IVT set 1/set 2 9-mer signals) must be scored with accuracy, precision, recall, and AUC and checked against published values. ([eduardoghi/reverse-engineering-skills](https://github.com/eduardoghi/reverse-engineering-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/binary-patch) · ★3)

- **binary-patcher**: Use when a lane needs binary patcher: Expert methodology for binary patching and bypass development. - **binary-patching**: Patch, bypass, or reverse-engineer a compiled binary without source. Reproduce and read ground truth first, trace the failing path under a debugger, find the split point where two code paths diverge, patch the narrowest site, re-sign, and verify three times. Use when the user wants to remove a check or limit, crack a license, change binary behaviour, or explain why an app, game, plugin, or library does X. ([ioma8/binary-patching](https://github.com/ioma8/binary-patching/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **binary-patching**: Use when patching binaries in a lab on authorized targets: your own software, CTF binaries, or client approved test builds. Covers byte patching, assembly level fixes, and verifying patches. Also use when demonstrating a fix for a found vulnerability. ([mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal](https://github.com/mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal/tree/HEAD/skills/binary/binary-patching))

- **binary-patching-techniques**: Use when you need to modify a compiled binary's behavior in an authorized lab, covering bypassing a license or trial check, neutralizing an anti-analysis routine, correcting a buggy code path, or testing how a program reacts to altered logic. Covers reading disassembly to find the target branch, NOP and jump patching, binary diffing to locate changes between versions, keygenning concepts for license validation analysis, and verifying patches safely. Use when dynamic analysis is blocked by a check you are authorized to bypass in your own lab, or when comparing two binary versions. ([mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal](https://github.com/mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal/tree/HEAD/skills/binary/binary-patching-techniques))
- **binary-patching-techniques-binary-diffing**: Use when you need to find exactly what changed between two binary versions, covering a vendor's security patch, a malware author's update, or your own patch verification. Covers byte-level diffing, function- level diffing with Diaphora and Ghidra Version Tracking, interpreting similarity scores, locating vulnerability fixes in patched binaries, and tracking malware evolution across variants. Use when a patch Tuesday binary needs analysis, when comparing malware variants, or when verifying your own patches changed only what was intended. ([mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal](https://github.com/mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal/tree/HEAD/skills/binary/binary-patching-techniques/sub/binary-patching-techniques-binary-diffing))
- **binary-patching-techniques-keygenning**: Use when you are authorized to analyze a software license validation routine, covering locating the check, understanding the algorithm, and assessing its strength. Covers finding validation code via strings and API references, reading checksum versus cryptographic versus server-side schemes, reimplementing the check for analysis, and writing the hardening assessment. Use only during authorized security assessments of your own or a client's software with explicit written permission. Never for bypassing licensing on software you are not authorized to assess. ([mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal](https://github.com/mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal/tree/HEAD/skills/binary/binary-patching-techniques/sub/binary-patching-techniques-keygenning))
- **binary-patching-techniques-nop-and-jump-patching**: Use when you need the precise mechanics of NOP and jump patching in x86/x64 binaries, covering instruction encodings, calculating padding, flipping conditional jumps, converting calls to NOP sleds, and patching in x64dbg versus on disk. Covers one-byte flips, multi- byte NOP fills, relative offset math, and verifying patches did not corrupt neighboring code. Use when neutralizing a check in an authorized lab, when learning patch mechanics, or when a patch must be byte-exact for reproducibility. ([mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal](https://github.com/mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal/tree/HEAD/skills/binary/binary-patching-techniques/sub/binary-patching-techniques-nop-and-jump-patching))

- **bingbingxiaomei-perspective**: 冰冰小美的思维框架与表达方式。基于8497条帖子、100+篇专栏文章、47个Wiki页面的深度调研， 提炼6个核心心智模型、8条决策启发式和完整的表达DNA。 用途：作为A股/港股/宏观交易的思维顾问，用冰冰小美的视角分析市场情绪、判断流动性走向、审视交易决策。 当用户提到「用冰美的视角」「冰美会怎么看」「冰美模式」「冰冰小美 perspective」时使用。 即使用户只是说「帮我用冰美的角度想想」「如果冰美会怎么做」「切换到冰美」也应触发。 触发词：冰美、冰冰小美、情绪交易、三要素、流动性分析 ([danieldengrt-commits/bingbingxiaomei-kb](https://github.com/danieldengrt-commits/bingbingxiaomei-kb/tree/HEAD/skills/bingbingxiaomei-perspective) · ★4)

- **bingbingxiaomei-workflow**: 分析冰冰小美的日常工作流，搭建冰美的信息处理系统。 分析她看什么信息，她怎么搭建她的工作，新闻/利率/汇率/央妈文件。 用途：帮助用户理解冰美的信息获取和处理方式，搭建类似的工作流。 触发词：冰美工作流、冰美看什么、冰美信息源、冰美怎么分析 ([danieldengrt-commits/bingbingxiaomei-kb](https://github.com/danieldengrt-commits/bingbingxiaomei-kb/tree/HEAD/skills/bingbingxiaomei-workflow) · ★4)

- **bingo-design**: Design UI on the Bingo canvas with senior product-design judgment. - **bio-03-wgcna**: WGCNA 加权基因共表达网络分析。当用户需要从表达矩阵中识别共表达模块、 选择软阈值、绘制模块-表型相关性热图、提取感兴趣模块基因列表时，使用此技能。 ([Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp](https://github.com/Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp/tree/HEAD/skills/original-sop/bio-03-wgcna))
- **bio-04-deg-limma**: 基于 limma 的差异表达基因分析。当用户需要在两组条件间筛选差异表达基因、 绘制火山图与聚类热图时，使用此技能。 ([Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp](https://github.com/Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp/tree/HEAD/skills/original-sop/bio-04-deg-limma))
- **bio-05-enrichment**: 差异基因功能富集分析。当用户需要对差异基因列表进行 GO (BP/CC/MF) 和 KEGG 通路富集分析、生成气泡图/条形图/通路图时，使用此技能。 ([Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp](https://github.com/Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp/tree/HEAD/skills/original-sop/bio-05-enrichment))
- **bio-06-gene-intersection**: 多基因集交集运算与韦恩图绘制。当用户需要将 WGCNA 模块基因与差异表达基因取交集、 筛选候选 Hub 基因时，使用此技能。 ([Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp](https://github.com/Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp/tree/HEAD/skills/original-sop/bio-06-gene-intersection))
- **bio-07-ml-lasso**: LASSO 惩罚回归特征筛选。当用户需要从候选基因中通过 L1 正则化筛选 最优特征子集、绘制回归系数路径图和交叉验证曲线时，使用此技能。 ([Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp](https://github.com/Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp/tree/HEAD/skills/original-sop/bio-07-ml-lasso))
- **bio-08-ml-randomforest**: 随机森林变量重要性分析。当用户需要通过随机森林和置换检验评估候选基因 的分类重要性（MeanDecreaseGini）、筛选显著特征基因时，使用此技能。 ([Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp](https://github.com/Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp/tree/HEAD/skills/original-sop/bio-08-ml-randomforest))
- **bio-09-hub-literature**: Hub 基因确定与科学文献证据挖掘。当用户需要取 LASSO 与随机森林双算法交集 确定最终关键基因、并通过 Scite 等学术数据库检索文献支撑其生物学意义时， 使用此技能。 ([Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp](https://github.com/Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp/tree/HEAD/skills/original-sop/bio-09-hub-literature))
- **bio-10-biomarker-roc**: 诊断标志物 ROC 曲线验证。当用户需要评估筛选出的 Hub 基因作为诊断标志物 的效能、绘制 ROC 曲线并计算 AUC 值时，使用此技能。 ([Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp](https://github.com/Sheldon-Bacchus/bio-spec-mvp/tree/HEAD/skills/original-sop/bio-10-biomarker-roc))
- **bio-admet-prediction**: Predicts ADMET properties using ADMETlab 3.0 API or DeepChem models. Estimates bioavailability, CYP inhibition, hERG liability, and 119 toxicity endpoints with uncertainty quantification. Filters for PAINS and other structural alerts. Use when filtering compounds for drug-likeness or prioritizing leads by predicted safety. ([FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bio-admet-prediction) · ★3.1k · 16 copies)
- **bio-admet-prediction**: Predicts ADMET properties using ADMETlab 3.0 (119 platform features, including 77 prediction models with modeled-endpoint uncertainty), ADMET-AI, DeepChem MolNet, and chemprop D-MPNN with explicit handling of OECD QSAR principles, applicability domain assessment, calibration, hERG/CYP/AMES endpoints, and PAINS / Lipinski / Ro5 / Veber / BBB druglikeness filters. Use when filtering compounds for drug-likeness, prioritizing leads by predicted safety, or building an in-house ADMET QSAR model. ([PKU-YuanGroup/OpenAI4S](https://github.com/PKU-YuanGroup/OpenAI4S/tree/HEAD/skills/bioskills/bio-chemoinformatics-admet-prediction) · ★671 · 8 copies)
- **bio-admet-prediction**: Predicts ADMET properties using ADMETlab 3.0 (119 endpoints with uncertainty), ADMET-AI, DeepChem MolNet, and chemprop D-MPNN with explicit handling of OECD QSAR principles, applicability domain assessment, calibration, hERG/CYP/AMES gold-standard endpoints, and PAINS / Lipinski / Ro5 / Veber / BBB druglikeness filters. Use when filtering compounds for drug-likeness, prioritizing leads by predicted safety, or building an in-house ADMET QSAR model. ([BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills](https://github.com/BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bioskills/admet-prediction) · ★224 · 3 copies)
- **bio-admet-prediction**: Predicts common small-molecule ADMET properties with an executable offline ADMET-AI route, optional current ADMETlab 3.0 service results, chemprop D-MPNN, and rule-based drug-likeness filters. Includes applicability-domain, calibration, hERG/CYP/AMES, and structural-alert safeguards. Use for lead triage or in-house ADMET QSAR work; not for clinical or regulatory conclusions from a single prediction. ([mrsonord2240/optimized-scientific-skills](https://github.com/mrsonord2240/optimized-scientific-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bio-admet-prediction) · ★1)

- **bio-agent**: Design AI agents with emotional metabolism — dopamine (interest), cortisol (stress), ATP (energy) as internal state. Use when the user says "bio agent", "agent metabolism", "emotional AI", "biological computationalism", "agent with feelings", "agent personality", "affective agent", or wants to give an AI agent internal emotional dynamics that influence its behavior. Based on Milinkovic & Aru (2025). ([master5d/claude-personal-skills](https://github.com/master5d/claude-personal-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bio-agent))
- **bio-agent-skills-hub**: Discover and invoke 1,676 deduplicated biomedical AI agent skills from the Awesome Bio Agent Skills repository (20 source repos, 15 categories). Use this skill as a router whenever a user needs a bioinformatics/biomedical task (genomics, transcriptomics, single-cell, proteomics, protein design, clinical, epigenomics, multi-omics, pathway, metagenomics, database queries, visualization, workflows): search the index, locate the best-matching skill, fetch its SKILL.md, and follow it. ([BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills](https://github.com/BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bio-agent-skills-hub) · ★224 · 2 copies)
- **bio-ai-product-manager**: help brainstorm ai chatbot and ai product ideas, analyze biology-related user needs, and draft product requirements documents, user stories, acceptance criteria, and api-aware feature requirements. use when the user wants product strategy help, prd drafting, requirement generation, roadmap thinking, or product discovery for applications and api services in biology or life science contexts. ([jonkiky/agentskills](https://github.com/jonkiky/agentskills/tree/HEAD/skills/bio-ai-product-manager) · 2 copies)
- **bio-alignment**: Align biological sequences and quantify how similar they are with Seqeron (MCP tools OR the C# API). Use for pairwise alignment (global/Needleman-Wunsch, local/Smith-Waterman, semi-global/fitting/overlap), multiple sequence alignment (MSA) + consensus/conservation, percent identity / similarity / gap %, edit (Levenshtein) and Hamming distance, k-mer similarity, longest common region, approximate matching, and prompts like "align these sequences", "how similar are X and Y", "find the conserved region", "does this query fit in that reference". Servers: alignment + core. ([AlexanderV/Seqeron](https://github.com/AlexanderV/Seqeron/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/bio-alignment) · ★5 · 2 copies)
